# Prior-detection sweep, round 2: the 26 on-target all-sky round-2 objects (TIC 183374279, TIC 61310956 and TIC 258006047 at full depth)

Generated 2026-10-19T13:18:00Z by `results/prior_search_round2/scripts/build_report_r2.py` from the JSONL query logs in `results/prior_search_round2/log/`. Every statement of absence below is backed by a logged query (section 2). Three objects (TIC 183374279, TIC 61310956, TIC 258006047) were swept at the full round-1 standard (every service, every identifier, live literature and web searches, GitHub code search, Zenodo content grep, new Zenodo deposits since 2026-10-19 00:00 UTC); the other 23 objects of `results/allsky_classA_round2_outcomes.csv` were swept with the batch services (ExoFOP TOI/CTOI tables and target pages for target + neighbours, SPOC TCE tables, TESS-SPOC DV scripts, NASA Exoplanet Archive, MAST products, SIMBAD, VizieR all-catalog + named-catalog cones, Gaia DR3 variability, VSX, ZTF, the cached Zenodo / GitHub / arXiv / T16 corpora, Zenodo metadata search, OpenAlex / Crossref / arXiv API, GitHub code search by TIC number, and git log -S in the full-history clones of the six key independent-candidate repositories); batch rows carry the stream prefix `b26_`. Round 1 (five earlier signals) is `results/prior_search/PRIOR_SEARCH.md`; its downloaded corpora (Zenodo text, GitHub clones, arXiv sources, TESS-SPOC scripts, T16 tables) were re-grepped read-only for the three new objects, and every live service was queried again.

Section order: 0 totals, 1 verdicts, 3 identifiers, 4 neighbour hits, 5 failures and gaps, then 2 (the full query log) as the final appendix. Superseded rows (Zenodo creator searches that hit a size=50 validation error or the 30/min rate limit before the s10d/s10e retries; the VSX call whose empty-list response was re-parsed in s14c) are kept in the log, tagged, and excluded from the totals.

## 1. Summary table (verdicts)

Period matching convention: a catalogued period counts as a match if it equals P, 2P, P/2, 3P or P/3 within 1 % (this covers the stated aliases 23.476 / 5.869 d for TIC 183374279 and 33.076 / 8.269 d for TIC 61310956). "Prior detection" means any public record of a transit-like or periodic signal on the target TIC, or on any TIC/Gaia source within 60 arcsec, at a matching period, dated before the author's own first public record of that object. Own-record cut-offs are per object: **TIC 183374279** -- GitHub fergmolina/tess-transit-candidates tag v1.6 and Zenodo 23474130 / 23474138, 2026-10-19 12:16 UTC (pre-registered candidate); **TIC 61310956** -- a machine-readable row (P = 16.537888 d, depth 863 ppm) in `tables/allsky_testable_soon.csv` of the same repository at tags v1.3, v1.4 and v1.5 and in Zenodo 23460915 / 23461948 / 23463569, public since 2026-10-18 23:24 UTC and removed from the table at v1.6 (2026-10-19 12:16 UTC); it was never a named or pre-registered candidate; **TIC 258006047** -- the same table row (P = 10.022287 d, depth 717 ppm) since 2026-10-18 23:24 UTC, and the rediscovery statement in the v1.6 release. The author's own records (Zenodo concept DOIs 23443244, 23462433, 23462437, 23462441 and their 13 versions) were found by the Zenodo, GitHub and offline layers and are tagged OWN in the log; they are not prior detections.

Neighbour variables: Gaia DR3 5848783938531699968 (28.5 arcsec, G 19.0) is a Gaia/VSX eclipsing binary with P = 0.236017 d, and Gaia DR3 5848784007295243392 (29.7 arcsec, G 16.9) a VSX VAR with P = 0.0871 d -- neither matches P or its aliases. | Zenodo 23230562 (2026-10-08 06:06 UTC; code file 2026-10-07 22:51); Zenodo 23286412 (2026-10-10 18:07 UTC) and 23293798 (2026-10-11 07:26 UTC) |

**Statement for the rediscovery note on TIC 258006047.** The 10.022-d signal on TIC 258006047 was first listed publicly by B. Murray in the frozen search code of Zenodo record 10.5281/zenodo.23230562 (created 2026-10-08 06:06 UTC; P = 10.022272 +- 0.00003 d, depth 761 ppm, duration 2.7 h, 2.1 Earth radii, with the 3.6-arcsec G = 15.9 background star flagged as the main alternative host), and mentioned without parameters as "under separate investigation" by R. Barve in Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.23286412 (2026-10-10 18:07 UTC; v2 23293798, 2026-10-11). Our own first public record of the star is a table row of 2026-10-18 23:24 UTC. No other record exists: it is not a TOI, CTOI or community candidate, has no SPOC or TESS-SPOC TCE, is absent from every candidate catalog queried, from 182 Zenodo records, from 390 logged GitHub queries: code search plus full-history git log -S on the cached repositories; hits on this number are digit-string collisions or catalog/coverage rows, not signal listings GitHub repositories and from the literature indices. The star itself is the ASAS-SN rotational variable ASASSN-V J144415.97-664747.4 (P = 10.655 d), 6 % from the transit period.

**Ecosystem note (update).** Murray's 10-candidate list also contains TIC 402710280 (deposited separately by R. Vora, Zenodo 23390316 / 23466634, 2026-10-15 and 2026-10-19), TIC 231694211, TIC 333735955 and TIC 32636748, which also appear in our own all-sky tables. Round 2 found 20 further October-2026 Zenodo candidate deposits by other independent searchers (Vora, Gold, Wanjare, Thawani, Morawski, Brown, Rege Nero, Ozturk, Dehorty, Krone, Zarco, Stinga); all were grepped and none mentions TIC 183374279 or TIC 61310956.

Not covered / limitations (explicit):
- TESS-SPOC DV (FFI TCE) products exist only for Sectors 36-85 and the S56-S69 multi-sector run (no new download scripts since round 1: index re-read and S86-S114 probed, all HTTP 404). Sectors of our detections inside that range, hence checked for a TESS-SPOC TCE: TIC 183374279 S69; TIC 61310956 S45, S46, S72; TIC 258006047 S38, S39, S65 -- none has a TESS-SPOC TCE. Outside the range (no FFI alert possible): TIC 183374279 S2, S29, S96, S103, S105; TIC 61310956 S22; TIC 258006047 S11, S12, S99-S102. QLP publishes no TCE list. SPOC 2-minute data exist for TIC 183374279 (S29) and TIC 61310956 (S22, S45): the SPOC TCE tables of those sectors and all multi-sector runs contain no TCE on either star (341,351 rows scanned).
- GitHub code search has index lag and covers only indexed repositories: the exact phrase "TIC 183374279" returns our own repository only after indexing; absence in code search is weak evidence. The grep of the 102 round-1 clones (read-only) plus the round-2 clones (section 2, streams r2_offline_grep and s11b) is the real check.
- ExoFOP observing notes attached to targets are visible only to logged-in users; all three targets have 0 notes, 0 files and 0 observations, so nothing was hidden.
- Semantic Scholar: HTTP 429 on every call in both passes (fast and 12-s spaced); not covered.
- ASAS-SN Sky Patrol v2: only the master_list catalog accepted cone searches (asassn_variables / variables return "invalid catalog"); the ASAS-SN variable-star catalogue is covered through VSX (AAVSO API and VizieR B/vsx) and VizieR II/366.
- NASA Exoplanet Archive cone queries fail (HTTP 403 via sync, ORA-00904 HTM20 via pyvo); the by-TIC queries (toi, ps, pscomppars, stellarhosts, k2pandc) for every target and neighbour TIC succeeded and are the ones that matter.
- NASA ADS full-text search: no API token on this machine and the web UI is a JavaScript shell; NOT performed. Substitutes: SIMBAD bibliography (CDS object-literature link, working ADQL: 4 / 3 / 2 references, all survey data releases or catalogue papers with no variability or transit content), arXiv API (metadata) plus full-text grep of 29 catalog-paper source tarballs (round-1 cache) and 20 further 2026 TESS candidate papers, OpenAlex, Crossref, Google Scholar (per-TIC: "did not match any articles"; two of the three pages also carried a service-error banner and are marked partial).
- arXiv full text: the arXiv API indexes title/abstract/author/comment fields only; full text was covered for the catalog papers above.
- Zenodo: the search API indexes metadata only, so every record created since 2026-10-19 00:00 UTC matching 17 TESS/transit/TIC/exoplanet query strings (25 records) and every related record surfaced by creator-name searches (Murray, Barve, Vora, Gold, Rabtsevich, Zarco, Tovar Contreras, Chopra, Fraser, Ozturk, Morawski, Wanjare, Thawani; 20 records) had its files downloaded and grepped; the 157 round-1 records were re-grepped from the cached text. Records outside those query terms could be missed. Zenodo rate limit (30/min) caused HTTP 429 on one block, which was retried after 60 s.
- Planet Hunters TESS Talk: the Zooniverse Talk search and comments API endpoints return 404 and the Talk web page is a JavaScript shell; the PHT candidate pipeline posts its candidates as CTOIs on ExoFOP (0 CTOIs on all three targets and on all 254 neighbour TICs).
- ZTF: TIC 183374279 (Dec -39) and TIC 258006047 (Dec -67) are south of the ZTF footprint; TIC 61310956 (Dec +16) has one ZTF object within 10 arcsec with only 6 zr epochs (star near the ZTF saturation limit), so ZTF cannot have detected the transit.
- Social media (X/Twitter, Reddit, Cloudy Nights) only via the web search engine; no mention found.
- HATSouth, NGTS, WASP, KELT, MASCARA have no public candidate lists beyond the VizieR tables queried (all 19 SuperWASP, 13 WASP, 37 KELT, 8 HATNet, 11 MASCARA, 6 Evryscope, 6 ASAS, 3 NSVS, 4 Catalina, 3 ATLAS, 1 ZTF VizieR catalogs found by Vizier.find_catalogs were cone-searched at 60 arcsec: no row on any target except NSVS photometry (no variability) for TIC 61310956).
- The round-1 GitHub clones were re-grepped as cached (read-only, state of 2026-10-18 ~20:40 UTC); repositories pushed after their cached clone and all newly discovered repositories were re-cloned in round 2 (stream s11b), and the six key ecosystem repositories (eumemic, brahle, zarco, ahrazzle, chopra, samsonmaximus) were cloned with full history and searched with git log -S.
